# Archive

Entries before 2026-10-20 are in [ARCHIVE-to-2026-10-19.md](ARCHIVE-to-2026-10-19.md), searched the same way. New entries go at the top, under this line.

- T-3053 (2026-11-10, agent termstop): trains stopped halfway into a platform at a line's end that is a through station (the drawing clamped the train inside the run, so its front stopped at the stop, the platform's middle), and with a terminal built they ran on into two drawn sidings past the platform. Now a train stands whole on the platform with its middle at the stop (over the line's end by up to half its length less half the platform; a long train overhangs on the entry side), a terminal's trains take the left or right platform track, and no siding or train is drawn past the last station. Drawing only (`terminalBays.ts`, `network.ts`, `rendererShaders.ts`, `trainGuard.ts`); the sim still counts the two turnback places for capacity. Check `checks/t3053-terminal-stop.mjs`.
- T-3055 [P2] (2026-11-10, agent greenmatch): green tram streets the snap can't use. The hint drew every service way, but the street graph keeps one only as a link of at most 60 m with both ends on a street (`joinRoads`), so long service roads round water works and car parks were green and never snapped (81 service lines in the Sakura-ku tile, 2 kept). `busRoadFilter` (map/busRoads.ts, used by the bus and tram hints) now draws only the bus classes (plus motorways for the bus hint); the few working links go undrawn. Check: checks/t3055-green-hint.mjs, artifacts/trainworld/t3055-before.png and t3055-after.png.
- T-3054 [P1] (2026-11-10, agent vehname): buses and trams called trains. Vehicle panel, map hover, schedule, alerts, trip route hovers, Construct and money hovers now say train, tram, bus or boat by the line's mode (`game/vehicle.ts`: `vehicleOf`, `nameBeforeNoun`); a line name that starts with its own mode's word drops it before the noun ("Bus 53" reads "53 bus"). The map hover took a train's size for any vehicle (1/240 on a bus; a bus is 80, a tram section 240); it now uses `vehicleCars`. Keys changed or added in all ten languages.

- T-3021, T-3022 (2026-11-10, agent loopend, tree loopend, 8c02a9a3): loop back at a line's end. In stop editing the stop before a loop of track at an end says "Click to loop back here"; the click turns the line one way round the loop with the stem served both ways (one edit, one undo), either end or both; on a turned line it says "Click to stop looping back here". Offered when the round trip comes out at most 120 s or 10% longer. "Click to close the loop" now only closes a ring; a loop's closing hop carries on round only within 300 s. Splits, joins, upgrades and undo keep the earliest day; older saves read as before recording began. New WASM functions: `set_game_day`, `edge_built_days`, `station_built_days`, `line_built_days`. - T-2867 Firefox out of memory on 16 GB (2026-11-09, agent firefox2, tree firefox2, not landed when written): reproduced in 32-bit Firefox 159 with the player's exact 57,318,568 bytes. A 32-bit browser is detected by a resizable ArrayBuffer's 4 GB maximum (its user agent says Win64) and gets 1 solving worker, no focus pool, smaller memory budgets; New York's real save reaches its final there, 12,218,770 rail trips. Any browser's worker that cannot get memory halves the pool and carries on. The memory failure offers "Play without intercity trips" (this tab; 174 MB less in a solving worker on New York). notes/T-2867.md, checks/t2867-memory-fallback.mjs. T-2868 filed.

- T-2793 Fares and riders falling to near 0 after an edit (2026-11-09, agent dips, Anita's TODO): reproduced on master with a repeatable check. The cause: the zone in view refined aside (T-2605) while a long line's stops had changed. `publishRefine` gave that line one zone's riders, and `refineOver` put them back over the local result and the whole final, so once the local result cleared "calculating" the 2% figure showed and was paid, unmarked. Tokyo Hikari (1,068 km): fares a day $1,296M to $19.9M, riders shown 548,839 to 37,970, income posted 281.5 to 4.5, for about 110 s. Now a zone-only solve never replaces a line that has figures (`withZone`, `publishRefine`, `publishNewLines` in workers/demandClient.ts; `?perfOff=keepFigures` restores the old route for comparison). After: worst line 94.9% of before; `checks/t2793-figure-dips.mjs lodEdit` and the other edit types pass on Tokyo, North America and a cached fresh tab. Smoke 26/26. No player-facing text changed. notes/T-2793.md.

- T-2820 A save with a broken line would not open (2026-11-09, agent crashfix, player report): a line left broken by deleting its track was saved with an edge id that read back past every array, so the next load panicked in `encode` at `keep()`, and the worker carried on with the stopped model until "recursive use" reached the player. `decode` now points such references at one dead slot, as in the running game, so these saves open; a corrupted file's bad edge ends are a damaged save. A panic while a game opens replaces the model, the player gets the real Rust message (`clockWorker.savePanic`, `last_panic()` in the track module) and the game goes on. His own save was not available; the cause is inferred from an exact match of error and trace. In tree crashfix; the WASM must be rebuilt when it lands (new export). notes/T-2820.md.

- T-2763 Edits that wait on the clock worker (2026-11-07, agent editlag): on the real save a placed station took 1.9-3.7 s to show (the split piece makes every line over it lay its strokes again), a blueprint delete 0.1-3.6 s, a built station delete 0.4-3.9 s; a money refusal is 4-40 ms in the worker, so its wait is the stop edit before it. - T-2716 The release-profile track module aborting at load (2026-11-07, agents boarding and heapfix, tree `boarding`): no overflow and no heap corruption. LLVM (GVN, LTO) folded dlmalloc's inlined size check, which reads the chunk header before a block, to 0 where the block came from a visible `__rust_alloc_zeroed` call; T-2699/T-1594's grow-ahead pad made the allocator too big to inline and left that shape (`geom::fit_capped`, `building_quote`). `sim/src/heap.rs` is now dlmalloc over its own system side growing in big steps, methods `#[inline(never)]`; the demand module uses it too. Tests, smoke, dumps pass; boarding steps p50 4-6 ms on all Japan. notes/T-2716.md.

Memory ruled out. notes/T-950.md.
